review_id: 5628
entity_slug: islander-food-shack
google_review_name: places/ChIJw3izHm2vkIgRmligHFjIQfg/reviews/Ci9DQUlRQUNvZENodHljRjlvT2s5eVVteHNSRGRaWWpCRU5tNXpWMHd0Vm5KTFFtYxAB
rating: 5
review_text: Such a fun place! It's a fabulous little shack across from the beach. Bright, vibrant decor and groovy beach vibe music playing.  Think Bob Marley and 311.

Both indoor and outdoor seating available,  but you're at the beach,  so indoors would just be silly.

The service was great,  and the food is really good.  I had the seafood platter with fries and a side salad.  Hubs had the whitefish basket with fries and slaw. The salad came out first, which was great, because I was starving.

The prices are really good compared to similar joints in the region,  with no sacrifice on service,  quality, or ambiance.

Our waitress was Shannon,  and she was so kind, thoughtful, and pleasant.

Will definitely go again.

--- entity_reviews [row 1 of 5] ---
id: f294bc4f-7c70-47d8-a0c5-6857c5d5bbc7
entity_slug: islander-food-shack
reviewer_name: Christy Dunaway
rating: 5
body: Such a fun place! It's a fabulous little shack across from the beach. Bright, vibrant decor and groovy beach vibe music playing.  Think Bob Marley and 311.

Both indoor and outdoor seating available,  but you're at the beach,  so indoors would just be silly.

The service was great,  and the food is really good.  I had the seafood platter with fries and a side salad.  Hubs had the whitefish basket with fries and slaw. The salad came out first, which was great, because I was starving.

The prices are really good compared to similar joints in the region,  with no sacrifice on service,  quality, or ambiance.

Our waitress was Shannon,  and she was so kind, thoughtful, and pleasant.

Will definitely go again.

entity_slug: islander-food-shack
reviewer_name: David Rowe
rating: 5
body: Islander Food Shack is a fantastic little spot that delivers on everything you want from a beachside eatery. The food is excellent—fresh, flavorful, and clearly made with care—and the service is just as good. Everyone is friendly, attentive, and genuinely welcoming.
The place itself is quaint and laid-back, with a relaxed atmosphere that fits perfectly with its location just across from the beach. It’s the kind of spot where you can grab a great meal, enjoy the vibe, and feel like you’ve found a local gem rather than a tourist trap.
Great food, great service, and a charming setting make Islander Food Shack a must-stop when you’re in the area.
